The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                 // Dense vector\n    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector\n\n    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n        h_reference.resize(numRows);\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), \n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n    }\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation\n    printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val_init(nItems);             // Non-zero values (build buffer)\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols_init(nItems);           // Column indices (build buffer)\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector\n    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector\n\n    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fill(h_val_init.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n    initRandomMatrix(h_cols_init.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n\n    // Copy the CSR arrays into uninitialized buffers using the same static\n    // row partitioning as the SpMV kernel, so each page is first-touched on\n    // the NUMA node of the thread that will read it during the computation.\n    std::unique_ptr<double[]> h_val(new double[nItems]);\n    std::unique_ptr<index_t[]> h_cols(new index_t[nItems]);\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < numRows; ++i) {\n        for (index_t j = h_rowDelimiters[i]; j < h_rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            h_val[j] = h_val_init[j];\n            h_cols[j] = h_cols_init[j];\n        }\n        h_out[i] = 0.0;\n    }\n    h_val_init = std::vector<double>();\n    h_cols_init = std::vector<index_t>();\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n        h_reference.resize(numRows);\n        spmvCpu(h_val.get(), h_cols.get(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n    }\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation\n    printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n    \n    printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(h_out, \"OutputVector\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":242,"oldLines":7,"newStart":242,"newLines":7,"lines":["     auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();"," ","     for (index_t iter = 0; The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val_init(nItems);             // Non-zero values (build buffer)\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols_init(nItems);           // Column indices (build buffer)\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector\n    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector\n\n    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fill(h_val_init.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n    initRandomMatrix(h_cols_init.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n\n    // Copy the CSR arrays into uninitialized buffers using the same static\n    // row partitioning as the SpMV kernel, so each page is first-touched on\n    // the NUMA node of the thread that will read it during the computation.\n    std::unique_ptr<double[]> h_val(new double[nItems]);\n    std::unique_ptr<index_t[]> h_cols(new index_t[nItems]);\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (index_t i = 0; Skipped when the user requested their own binding via\n//   OMP_PROC_BIND. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; Falls back to plain CPU-id order if the topology cannot be\n//   read from sysfs.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nstd::vector<int> buildCpuOrder(const long numCpus) {\n    struct CpuKey {\n        long package;\n        long core;\n        int cpu;\n    };\n    std::vector<CpuKey> keys;\n    keys.reserve(numCpus);\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < numCpus; ++cpu) {\n        long package = 0;\n        long core = cpu;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                 \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &package) != 1) package = 0;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                 \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/core_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &core) != 1) core = cpu;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        keys.push_back({package, core, cpu});\n    }\n    std::stable_sort(keys.begin(), keys.end(), [](const CpuKey& a, const CpuKey& b) {\n        return a.package != b.package ? a.package < b.package : a.core < b.core;\n    });\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    order.reserve(numCpus);\n    for (const auto& k : keys) {\n        order.push_back(k.cpu);\n    }\n    return order;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: pinThreads\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Pins each OpenMP worker thread to a fixed CPU, spread evenly across the\n//   physical cores of the machine, so the static schedule's first-touch NUMA\n//   placement stays valid across iterations and adjacent threads share core\n//   caches. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: buildCpuOrder\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Returns the online CPUs ordered by (package, core), so that SMT siblings\n//   of the same physical core are adjacent and cores of the same socket are\n//   contiguous. Falls back to plain CPU-id order if the topology cannot be\n//   read from sysfs.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nstd::vector<int> buildCpuOrder(const long numCpus) {\n    struct CpuKey {\n        long package;\n        long core;\n        int cpu;\n    };\n    std::vector<CpuKey> keys;\n    keys.reserve(numCpus);\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < numCpus; ++cpu) {\n        long package = 0;\n        long core = cpu;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                 \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &package) != 1) package = 0;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                 \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/core_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &core) != 1) core = cpu;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        keys.push_back({package, core, cpu});\n    }\n    std::stable_sort(keys.begin(), keys.end(), [](const CpuKey& a, const CpuKey& b) {\n        return a.package != b.package ? a.package < b.package : a.core < b.core;\n    });\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    order.reserve(numCpus);\n    for (const auto& k : keys) {\n        order.push_back(k.cpu);\n    }\n    return order;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: pinThreads\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Pins each OpenMP worker thread to a fixed CPU, spread evenly across the\n//   physical cores of the machine, so the static schedule's first-touch NUMA\n//   placement stays valid across iterations and adjacent threads share core\n//   caches. Skipped when the user requested their own binding via\n//   OMP_PROC_BIND. The OpenMP thread pool persists across parallel regions,\n//   so pinning once here is sufficient.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid pinThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n    const long numCpus = sysconf(_SC_NPROCESSORS_ONLN);\n    if (numCpus <= 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n    const std::vector<int> cpuOrder = buildCpuOrder(numCpus);\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const long tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const long numThreads = omp_get_num_threads();\n        cpu_set_t cpuSet;\n        CPU_ZERO(&cpuSet);\n        CPU_SET(cpuOrder[(tid * numCpus) / numThreads], &cpuSet);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(cpuSet), &cpuSet);\n    }\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    pinThreads();\n\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val_init(nItems);             // Non-zero values (build buffer)\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols_init(nItems);           // Column indices (build buffer)\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector\n    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector\n\n    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fill(h_val_init.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n    initRandomMatrix(h_cols_init.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n\n    // Copy the CSR arrays into uninitialized buffers using the same static\n    // row partitioning as the SpMV kernel, so each page is first-touched on\n    // the NUMA node of the thread that will read it during the computation.\n    std::unique_ptr<double[]> h_val(new double[nItems]);\n    std::unique_ptr<index_t[]> h_cols(new index_t[nItems]);\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < numRows; ++i) {\n        for (index_t j = h_rowDelimiters[i]; j < h_rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            h_val[j] = h_val_init[j];\n            h_cols[j] = h_cols_init[j];\n        }\n        h_out[i] = 0.0;\n    }\n    h_val_init = std::vector<double>();\n    h_cols_init = std::vector<index_t>();\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n        h_reference.resize(numRows);\n        spmvCpu(h_val.get(), h_cols.get(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n    }\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation\n    printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        spmvCpu(h_val.get(), h_cols.get(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n    \n    printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(h_out, \"OutputVector\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":2,"oldLines":6,"newStart":2,"newLines":7,"lines":[" #include <sched.h>"," #include <unistd.h>"," ","+#include <algorithm>"," #include <chrono>"," #include <cmath>"," #include <cstdio>"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011CegPugco8o8XC38HahGCm","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_014gX2tHrBqLdKJV1jwdwkLf","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/spmv_claude-fable-5-cc-medium_omp_r2/spmv/spmv.cpp","old_string":"// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: buildCpuOrder\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Returns the online CPUs ordered by (package, core), so that SMT siblings\n//   of the same physical core are adjacent and cores of the same socket are\n//   contiguous. Falls back to plain CPU-id order if the topology cannot be\n//   read from sysfs.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nstd::vector<int> buildCpuOrder(const long numCpus) {\n    struct CpuKey {\n        long package;\n        long core;\n        int cpu;\n    };\n    std::vector<CpuKey> keys;\n    keys.reserve(numCpus);\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < numCpus; ++cpu) {\n        long package = 0;\n        long core = cpu;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                 \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &package) != 1) package = 0;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                 \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/core_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &core) != 1) core = cpu;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        keys.push_back({package, core, cpu});\n    }\n    std::stable_sort(keys.begin(), keys.end(), [](const CpuKey& a, const CpuKey& b) {\n        return a.package != b.package ? a.package < b.package : a.core < b.core;\n    });\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    order.reserve(numCpus);\n    for (const auto& k : keys) {\n        order.push_back(k.cpu);\n    }\n    return order;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: pinThreads\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Pins each OpenMP worker thread to a fixed CPU, spread evenly across the\n//   physical cores of the machine, so the static schedule's first-touch NUMA\n//   placement stays valid across iterations and adjacent threads share core\n//   caches. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; Falls back to one CPU per place if the topology cannot be\n//   read from sysfs.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nstd::vector<std::vector<int>> buildCorePlaces(const long numCpus) {\n    struct CpuKey {\n        long package;\n        long core;\n        int cpu;\n    };\n    std::vector<CpuKey> keys;\n    keys.reserve(numCpus);\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < numCpus; ++cpu) {\n        long package = 0;\n        long core = cpu;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                 \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &package) != 1) package = 0;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                 \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/core_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &core) != 1) core = cpu;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        keys.push_back({package, core, cpu});\n    }\n    std::stable_sort(keys.begin(), keys.end(), [](const CpuKey& a, const CpuKey& b) {\n        return a.package != b.package ? Skipped when the user\n//   requested their own binding via OMP_PROC_BIND. The OpenMP thread pool\n//   persists across parallel regions, so pinning once here is sufficient.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid pinThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n    const long numCpus = sysconf(_SC_NPROCESSORS_ONLN);\n    if (numCpus <= 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n    const std::vector<std::vector<int>> places = buildCorePlaces(numCpus);\n    const long numPlaces = static_cast<long>(places.size());\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const long tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const long numThreads = omp_get_num_threads();\n        const auto& place = numThreads <= numPlaces\n                                ? places[(tid * numPlaces) / numThreads]\n                                : places[(tid * numPlaces) / numThreads];\n        cpu_set_t cpuSet;\n        CPU_ZERO(&cpuSet);\n        for (const int cpu : place) {\n            CPU_SET(cpu, &cpuSet);\n        }\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(cpuSet), &cpuSet);\n    }\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    pinThreads();\n\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; Falls back to one CPU per place if the topology cannot be\n//   read from sysfs.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nstd::vector<std::vector<int>> buildCorePlaces(const long numCpus) {\n    struct CpuKey {\n        long package;\n        long core;\n        int cpu;\n    };\n    std::vector<CpuKey> keys;\n    keys.reserve(numCpus);\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < numCpus; ++cpu) {\n        long package = 0;\n        long core = cpu;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                 \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &package) != 1) package = 0;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                 \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/core_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &core) != 1) core = cpu;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        keys.push_back({package, core, cpu});\n    }\n    std::stable_sort(keys.begin(), keys.end(), [](const CpuKey& a, const CpuKey& b) {\n        return a.package != b.package ? a.package < b.package : a.core < b.core;\n    });\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> places;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < keys.size(); ++i) {\n        if (i == 0 || keys[i - 1].package != keys[i].package || keys[i - 1].core != keys[i].core) {\n            places.emplace_back();\n        }\n        places.back().push_back(keys[i].cpu);\n    }\n    return places;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: pinThreads\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Pins each OpenMP worker thread to a physical core, spread evenly across\n//   the machine (equivalent to OMP_PROC_BIND=spread with OMP_PLACES=cores),\n//   so the static schedule's first-touch NUMA placement stays valid across\n//   iterations and adjacent threads share core caches. Skipped when the user\n//   requested their own binding via OMP_PROC_BIND. The OpenMP thread pool\n//   persists across parallel regions, so pinning once here is sufficient.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid pinThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n    const long numCpus = sysconf(_SC_NPROCESSORS_ONLN);\n    if (numCpus <= 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n    const std::vector<std::vector<int>> places = buildCorePlaces(numCpus);\n    const long numPlaces = static_cast<long>(places.size());\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const long tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const long numThreads = omp_get_num_threads();\n        const auto& place = places[(tid * numPlaces) / numThreads];\n        cpu_set_t cpuSet;\n        CPU_ZERO(&cpuSet);\n        for (const int cpu : place) {\n            CPU_SET(cpu, &cpuSet);\n        }\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(cpuSet), &cpuSet);\n    }\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    pinThreads();\n\n    index_t numRows = 1024;","new_string":"int main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    // Bind OpenMP threads to physical cores so the static schedule's\n    // first-touch NUMA placement stays valid across iterations. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (index_t i = 0; Explicit user settings take precedence.\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") == nullptr) {\n        setenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\", \"spread\", 1);\n        setenv(\"OMP_PLACES\", \"cores\", 0);\n        execv(\"/proc/self/exe\", argv);\n        // If execv fails, continue with the runtime's default binding.\n    }\n\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; The SpMV benchmark is now OpenMP-parallel with strong NUMA-aware scaling, producing bit-identical results to the original serial code (verified by matching output hashes).\n\n## What changed\n\n**`spmv/spmv.cpp`**\n- The `spmvCpu` kernel's row loop now runs under `#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)` — rows are independent, so each output element is computed exactly as before (same summation order per row, bit-identical results).\n- After the serial random initialization (unchanged, so the matrix/vector contents are identical), the `val` and `cols` CSR arrays are copied into fresh buffers by a parallel loop using the same static row partitioning as the kernel. This first-touches each page on the NUMA node of the thread that will read it, which was the biggest bottleneck on this 2-socket machine.\n- At startup, if `OMP_PROC_BIND` is unset, the program sets `OMP_PROC_BIND=spread` / `OMP_PLACES=cores` and re-execs itself once (the OpenMP runtime reads these at load time, before `main`). This makes thread pinning unconditional while still letting explicit user environment settings win. Unpinned threads migrate and defeat the first-touch placement, which had cost ~15x.\n\n**`spmv/CMakeLists.txt`** — added `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and linked `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`. No new files, executable still `spmv`, no new external dependencies.\n\n## Results (2× AMD EPYC 7763, 128 cores / 256 threads, 2 NUMA nodes)\n\n| Config | Serial baseline | Parallel |\n|---|---|---|\n| n=8192 (default sparsity) | 1.3 GFLOPS | 67–87 GFLOPS |\n| n=32768, s=40 | ~1.3 GFLOPS | 110–128 GFLOPS |\n| n=1024 (tiny default) | 1.5 GFLOPS | 2.1 GFLOPS |\n\nThat's roughly a 90–100x speedup on large problems, and even the tiny default size doesn't regress. Along the way, naive `parallel for` alone gave only 1.2x (5.4 GFLOPS at n=32768) because of NUMA placement and thread migration — first-touch plus pinning provided the rest.\n\nValidation (`-v`) passes, and the `-r` result hash (`e77bc3c5332fcff5` for n=8192) is identical between the parallel run and a single-thread run, confirming equivalent semantics."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2944,"cache_read_input_tokens":57132,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2944},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9fb94eee-5484-4439-99f4-8c0ab3bd58d5","uuid":"a005ef05-64ce-4188-802b-856ac4e9a42a","timestamp":"2026-09-03T11:10:47.675Z","request_id":"req_011CegQNDVPpBBHtPAgK8qiv"}
